Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in electronics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 759 and the invariant I(A,B) = 760 mod 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 759 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the electronics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 760. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
